公司簡介
Corporate Profile

                                           綠城中國控股有限公司是中國領先的優質房產品開發及生
                                           活綜合服務供應商，以優秀的產品質量、獨特的建築美
                                           學、以人為本的服務保持行業引領地位。公司連續二十
                                           年榮獲「中國房地產百強企業綜合實力TOP10」1 ，所進駐
                                           重點城市客戶滿意度連續十四年保持領先，連續五年位列
                                          「中國房地產產品力優秀企業」榜單第一 1 ，並獲得「中國房
                                           地產交付力優秀企業」
                                                    「 中國房地產年度社會責任感企業」
                                           等榮譽。

                                           Greentown China Holdings Limited is a leading quality
                                           property developer and an integrated living service provider
                                           in China. It maintains a leadership position in the industry by
                                           virtue of the quality of its properties, its unique architectural
                                           aesthetics, and its customer-centric services. The Company
                                           has been awarded the “Top10 among 100 Chinese Real
                                           Estate Enterprises by Comprehensive Strength” 1 for 20
                                           consecutive years and maintained a leading position in
                                           customer satisfaction in core cities in which it operates for 14
                                           consecutive years as well as ranked first among “Chinese Real
                                           Estate Enterprises with Excellent Product Strength”1 for five
                                           consecutive years. It has also received such honors as “China
                                           Excellent Real Estate Enterprises by Delivery Strength” and
                                           “China Real Estate Social Responsibility Enterprise of the
                                           Year”.


                                           綠城中國控股有限公司於1995年1月在中國杭州成立，2006年7月在香港聯交所上
                                           市，並先後引入九龍倉、中交集團作為重要股東。於本報告日期，綠城中國的最
                                           大單一股東中交集團持有本公司已發行股本總額約28.97%的權益。

                                           Greentown China Holdings Limited was founded in Hangzhou, China in January
                                           1995 and listed on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in July 2006. Wharf and CCCG
                                           were introduced as substantial shareholders successively. As at the date of this
                   天津水西雲廬                  report, CCCG, Greentown China’s single largest shareholder, was interested in
                   Tianjin Shuixi Yunlu    approximately 28.97% of the total issued share capital of the Company.


002   Greentown China Holdings Limited 綠城中國控股有限公司
公司簡介
Corporate Profile




歷經近三十年的發展，公司秉持「品質為先」的發     Throughout nearly 30 years of development, the Company has adhered to
展戰略指引，佈局房地產上下游業務，形成重資      the development strategy of “quality first” and developed the upstream and
產、輕資產和「綠城＋」三大板塊。重資產板塊聚     downstream of the real estate business to form three major segments, namely
焦房產開發業務，在傳統開發業務基礎上，積極      asset-heavy, asset-light and “Greentown+”. The asset-heavy segment focuses
發展特色房地產業務，包括小鎮、城市更新等相      on the real estate development business, and actively builds its featured real
關業務；輕資產板塊強化代建業務，2020年7月    estate businesses, including town projects, urban renewal projects and other
正式分拆綠城管理控股有限公司並於聯交所主板      businesses, on the foundation of its traditional development business. The
上市（股票代碼：09979），市佔率在行業中遙遙   asset-light segment strengthens the project management business. In July
領先；「綠城＋」板塊發展和培育不動產產業鏈業     2020, Greentown Management Holdings Company Limited was officially spun
務，建立產業生態圈，協同賦能主業。          off and listed separately on the Main Board of the Stock Exchange (stock code:
                           09979), with a market share ranking far ahead in the industry. The “Greentown+”
                           segment develops and cultivates the industry chain business of real estate
                           and establishes an industrial ecosystem to collaborate and empower its main
                           business.

作為中國精品物業營造專家，綠城中國一直堅持      Being a professional developer of premium properties in China, Greentown China
創新，不斷探索人居關係，在營造美麗建築的基      has always insisted on innovation and continued to explore the relationship
礎上，致力於為更多人打造美好生活。公司立足      between humans and their living space. Through creating aesthetically pleasing
於浙江，聚焦核心區域，深耕一二綫及優質三四      architecture, Greentown China is committed to building a better life for more
綫城市。綠城中國憑藉優質的開發品質、高質的      people. Based in Zhejiang, the Company focuses its attention on core areas as
土地儲備、高效的組織架構、優秀的管理團隊，      well as cultivating footprints in first- and second-tier cities and high quality third-
在業務所及的城市成功樹立品牌形象，並累積了      and fourth-tier cities. Leveraging its superior development quality, premium land
大量高質量項目的開發經驗和卓越的營運能力，      bank, highly effective organizational structure and high-calibre management
為可持續發展提供了強勁動力。             team, Greentown China has established its brand image in all the cities where
                           it operates. The rich experience that Greentown has accumulated in developing
                           numerous high-quality projects together with its outstanding operational
                           capabilities has served to provide strong momentum for its sustainable
                           development.

一直以來，公司堅守「真誠、善意、精緻、完美」     The Company has always adhered to its corporate values of “Sincerity, Kindness,
的企業價值觀，致力於以「高顏值、極賢惠、最      Exquisiteness, Perfection”, It is committed to building Greentown “Good Houses”
聰明，房低碳、全周期、人健康」為目標建設綠      with the goal of being “gorgeous, extremely virtuous, the smartest, low-carbon,
城「好房子」，不斷滿足人們對理想生活的追求。     full-cycle and healthy”, and constantly meets people’s pursuit of ideal life. In
2024年上半年，公司整體業績穩中有進，合同銷    the first half of 2024, the Company has achieved steady progress in overall
售額約人民幣1,265億元，位列行業第三，新增投   performance, with contract sales of approximately RMB126.5 billion, ranking third
資位列行業第四。                   in the industry, and newly added investment ranking fourth in the industry.


面向未來，公司將保持戰略定力，以打造「TOP10   Looking ahead, the Company will adhere to its strategic principle with the aim
中的品質標桿」為目標，以「最懂客戶、最懂產品」    of building the “quality benchmark among the Top10” and the strategic pivot
為戰略支點，持續踐行「以客戶為中心的產品主      points of “best understanding of customers and best understanding of products”,
義」，同時強化變革意識，全面提升能力，主動應     continue to implement its strategy of “customer-oriented product offering”,
對挑戰，積極抓住機遇，堅定走「全品質、高質      while strengthening the consciousness of change and uplifting its capacity in an
量、可持續」的發展道路。               all-round manner to respond to challenges and seize opportunities proactively,
                           and firmly insist on the path of “comprehensively high quality and sustainable”
                           development.

                        西安鳳鳴海棠
                        Xi’an Fengming Begonia
管理層討論與分析
Management Discussion and Analysis




經營回顧                                 Operation Review
2024年上半年，我國經濟平穩運行，實現5.0%             In the first half of 2024, China’s economy has maintained a stable operation,
的GDP增長。但房地產行業延續調整態勢，新房               achieving a GDP growth of 5.0%. However, the real estate industry continued its
市場仍處於築底階段。政策方面，延續寬鬆主基                adjustment trend, and the new housing market was still in the bottoming stage.
調，聚焦穩市場、去庫存，見效仍需時間；市場                In terms of policies, it would still take time to take effect under the main tone
方面，居民收入預期未明顯改善，新建商品房銷                of continued easing with the focus on stabilizing the market and destocking.
售額同比下降25.0%，成交規模處近年來同期低              In terms of the market, residents’ income expectations have not improved

匯率波動風險                                          Risks of foreign exchange fluctuation
本集團之營運所在地主要在中華人民共和國，絕                           The principal place of operation of the Group is in the PRC, and the majority
大部分的收入與支出均以人民幣結算。由於本集                           of its income and expenditure is settled in RMB. As the Group had deposits in
團存有外幣存款、外幣應收及應付關聯人士款項                           foreign currencies, amounts due from and to related parties and third parties
和第三方款項以及尚有外幣銀行借款和境外優先                           denominated in foreign currencies, as well as bank borrowings in foreign
票據，故本集團面對外匯風險。其中本期已通過                           currencies and overseas senior notes, the Group was exposed to foreign
提取境外人民幣貸款等值5.6億美元來提前置換境                         exchange risks. Among which, the Group made early replacement of offshore US
外美元貸款，扣除該部分後本公司外幣借款佔總                           dollar loans by withdrawal of offshore RMB loans equivalent to US$560 million
借貸佔比不高且匯率波動為階段性波動，本期本                           during the Period, excluding which, the share of foreign currency borrowing in
公司未訂立任何外匯對沖安排，本公司將繼續關                           the Company’s total borrowings was relatively low, and the foreign exchange
注外匯市場的變化，並積極與各大銀行探討外匯                           fluctuation was cyclical in nature. No foreign exchange hedging arrangements
對沖方案。本期產生匯兌淨損失人民幣0.74億元                         were entered into by the Company during the Period. The Company will continue
（2023年同期：人民幣6.93億元）。                            to closely monitor the foreign exchange market change and proactively discuss
                                                foreign exchange hedging proposals with all banks. Net foreign exchange loss of
                                                RMB74 million (the corresponding period of 2023: RMB693 million) was incurred

指明者則除外。行使價由董事會釐定，且將不少          specified in the 2023 Share Option Scheme. The exercise price is determined
於以下各項的最高者：(i)本公司股份於授出日期在       by the Board, and will not be less than the highest of (i) the closing price of
聯交所每日報價表所示的收市價；(ii)本公司股份       the Shares of the Company as stated in the Stock Exchange’s daily quotation
於緊接授出日期前5個營業日在聯交所每日報價表         sheet on the date of grant; (ii) the average of the closing prices of the Shares of
所示的平均收市價；及(iii)股份的面值。          the Company as stated in the Stock Exchange’s daily quotation sheets for five
                               business days immediately preceding the date of grant; and (iii) the nominal value
                               of the Shares.


於2024年6月30日，本公司並未根據2023年購股     As at 30 June 2024, no share options have been granted by the Company under
權計劃授出購股權。根據2023年購股權計劃可供        the 2023 Share Option Scheme. The total number of share options available for
授出的購股權總數為253,199,869份，佔本公司於    grant under the 2023 Share Option Scheme was 253,199,869, representing 10%
2023年購股權計劃的採納日期（即2023年6月16     of the issued share capital of the Company as at the date of adoption of the 2023
日）、2024年1月1日及2024年6月30日已發行股本   Share Option Scheme (i.e. 16 June 2023), 1 January 2024 and 30 June 2024.
的10%。

審閱範圍                        Scope of Review
我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審閱準則       We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong Standard on Review
第2410號由實體的獨立核數師審閱中期財務資料     Engagements 2410 Review of Interim Financial Information Performed by the
進行審閱。中期財務資料的審閱包括主要向負責       Independent Auditor of the Entity issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified
財務及會計事務的人員作出查詢，並應用分析性       Public Accountants. A review of interim financial information consists of making
及其他審閱程序。審閱範圍遠小於根據香港核數       inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial and accounting matters,
準則進行審核的範圍，故不能令我們保證我們將       and applying analytical and other review procedures. A review is substantially
知悉在審核中可能發現的所有重大事項。因此，       less in scope than an audit conducted in accordance with Hong Kong Standards
我們不會發表審核意見。                 on Auditing and consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that we
                            would become aware of all significant matters that might be identified in an audit.
                            Accordingly, we do not express an audit opinion.

            平值之間的差額計算得出。                                                                              value of the consideration paid or received for the partial acquisition or disposal.

            公司的董事會根據中華人民共和國（「中國」）有關法律                                                                 by the boards of directors of the relevant companies in the Group in accordance with the
            法規釐定。該儲備於取得有關機關批准後，可用於抵                                                                   relevant laws and regulations of the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”). This reserve can
            銷累計虧損及增加資本。                                                                               be used to offset accumulated losses and increase capital upon approval from the relevant

1.   公司資料                            1.   CORPORATE INFORMATION
     綠城中國控股有限公司（「本公司」）於2005               Greentown China Holdings Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated in
     年8月31日根據公司法（2004年修訂本）於               the Cayman Islands on 31 August 2005 as an exempted company with
     開曼群島註冊成立為一家獲豁免有限公                    limited liability under the Companies Law (2004 Revision) and its shares
     司，其股份於2006年7月13日於香港聯合                have been listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong
     交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。                  Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) with effect from 13 July 2006.


     本公司乃投資控股公司。其附屬公司（連同                  The Company is an investment holding company. The principal activity of
     本公司統稱為「本集團」）的主要業務為於中                 its subsidiaries (together with the Company referred to as the “Group”) is
     國發展供銷售住宅物業。                          the development for sale of residential properties in the PRC.

11.   股息                                       11.   DIVIDENDS
      於期內，本公司董事會（「董事會」）已建議                           During the period, a final dividend of RMB0.43 per ordinary share,
      宣派並於2024年6月14日由股東於股東週                          or RMB1,088,759,000 in total, for the year ended 31 December 2023
      年大會批准截至2023年12月31日止年度的                         (six months ended 30 June 2023: RMB0.5 per ordinary share, or

      人民幣1,088,759,000元（截至2023年6月30                  recommended by the board of the directors of the Company (the “Board”)
      日止六個月：宣派截至2022年12月31日止                         and approved by the shareholders at the annual general meeting on 14
      年度的末期股息每股普通股人民幣0.5元，                           June 2024. The final dividend was subsequently paid on 31 July 2024.

30.   金融工具的公平值及公平值等級                             30.      FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY OF
                                                          FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS
      管理層已評估，銀行結餘及現金、抵押銀                                  Management has assessed that the fair values of bank balances and
      行存款、貿易及其他應收款項、訂金及預                                  cash, pledged bank deposits, trade and other receivables, deposits and
      付款項、應收╱應付關聯人士款項、貿易                                  prepayments, amounts due from/to related parties, trade and other
      及其他應付款項以及銀行及其他借款流動                                  payables, the current portion of bank and other borrowings approximate
      部分的公平值與其賬面值相若，主要由於                                  to their carrying amounts largely due to the short-term maturities of these
      該等工具於短期內到期。                                         instruments.


      管理層負責釐定金融工具公平值計量的政                                  Management is responsible for determining the policies and procedures
      策及程序。於各報告日期，管理層分析金                                  for the fair value measurement of financial instruments. At each reporting
      融工具價值的變動情況，並釐定估值中所                                  date, management analyses the movements in the values of financial
      用的主要輸入數據。估值過程及結果經董                                  instruments and determines the major inputs applied in the valuation. The
      事會一年討論兩次，以進行中期及年度財                                  valuation process and results are discussed with the board of directors
      務匯報。                                                twice a year for interim and annual financial reporting.


      金融資產及負債的公平值計入自願雙方可                                  The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are included at
      於當前交易（脅迫或清盤銷售除外）中交換                                 the amount at which the instrument could be exchanged in a current
      該工具所需的金額。估計公平值使用以下                                  transaction between willing parties, other than in a forced or liquidation
      方法及假設：                                              sale. The following methods and assumptions were used to estimate the
                                                          fair values:


      按公平值計入損益的金融資產（指投資公司                                 The fair values of the financial assets at fair value through profit or loss,
      及銀行發行的理財產品）公平值按使用擁有                                 which represent wealth management products issued by investment
      類似條款、信貸風險及餘下到期期限的工                                  companies and banks, have been calculated by discounting the expected
      具目前可得的利率折讓預期未來現金流量                                  future cash flows using rates currently available for instruments with
      計算。於報告期末，本集團按公平值計入                                  similar terms, credit risk and remaining maturities. The Group’s own non-
      損益的金融資產的自身不履約風險獲評估                                  performance risk for financial assets at fair value through profit or loss as
      為不重大。                                               at the end of the reporting period was assessed to be insignificant.
